The 2026-06-26 Asset Browser placeholder follow-up keeps empty preview and secondary details surfaces low-emphasis. `template_style/colors/surface/variants.rs` now maps `asset-placeholder` and `asset-placeholder-visual` to the inset surface family without a border, and `template_style_tests/surface.rs` locks that behavior. This lets the selected table row and selected asset preview remain visible without painting cyan selection boxes around the right-side details placeholders.

The 2026-06-26 Asset Browser selected-preview follow-up keeps selected preview cards in the same low-emphasis selection language. `template_style/surface_roles.rs` centralizes the `asset-preview` and `asset-preview-visual` classification, `template_style/colors/surface/interaction.rs` resolves their selected/focused fill to `PALETTE.surface_pressed` instead of `PALETTE.surface_selected`, `template_style/dimensions.rs` preserves the authored 1 px border, and `template_style/colors/border.rs` keeps interactive preview-card borders on the normal muted border rather than the focus ring. `template_style_tests/surface.rs` locks the dark fill, muted outline, focus override, and validation override so the card no longer becomes a full cyan block or outline.

The 2026-06-26 Asset Browser thumbnail-placeholder follow-up introduced a dedicated image placeholder owner. The 2026-06-27 refinement keeps that owner but removes the noisy hand-drawn dot, ridge, shadow, and baseline vocabulary. `template_asset_placeholder_visuals.rs` now recognizes `asset-placeholder-visual` and `asset-preview-visual`, paints one recessed thumbnail well with no inner border, and centers the real `image` SVG asset through the existing `visual_assets` pipeline. The fallback pipeline still invokes this owner after the base surface and before optional image commands, so real image resources can replace the placeholder while plain `asset-placeholder` cards remain simple inset containers. Pixel and command regressions in the same module lock the single-well contract, real SVG pixels, selected preview visuals, and plain placeholders separately.

The later 2026-06-27 asset-type icon refinement keeps that same placeholder owner boundary. `template_asset_placeholder_visuals.rs` treats nodes with `component_role=asset-thumbnail-visual` as typed thumbnail wells and uses the node `component_variant` as the icon name before falling back to `icon_name` or `image`. The semantic names are resolved in `visual_assets/candidates/aliases.rs`, so `asset-texture`, `asset-material`, `asset-scene`, `asset-mesh`, `asset-shader`, and sibling variants load real SVG pixels through the existing visual-asset path. This keeps asset-type selection out of the root painter while allowing Asset Browser thumbnail and preview nodes to share one typed visual contract.

The follow-up typed-thumbnail quiet-canvas refinement keeps the same split. `template_asset_placeholder_visuals.rs` now paints typed thumbnail visuals as one recessed `surface_inset` well with one centered semantic SVG icon clamped to the 24-32 px shell range; it no longer adds a centered low-emphasis icon plate, a duplicate type badge, or a per-kind button-like surface. Plain generic `image` placeholders use the same relative 24-32 px edge clamp but stay single-tint and lower-emphasis. Type tinting stays local to the placeholder visual owner, while `visual_assets/candidates/aliases.rs` adds `asset-ui-layout`, `asset-ui-widget`, and `asset-ui-style` aliases so UI resources do not collapse to script/file symbols. The command regressions lock the recessed well color, icon size clamp, generic fallback size, and real-pixel alias resolution without adding a root painter branch.

The 2026-07-02 quiet-canvas pass validates this owner with scoped rustfmt, direct `asset_thumbnail_visual` 3/3, direct `thumbnail_view` 9/9, direct M3 screenshot harness 1/1, and an editor-host build in `D:\cargo-targets\zircon-editor-thumbnail-visual-0702`. Repo `target` same-name screenshot scan returned 0.

The follow-up thumbnail type-badge pass keeps tile metadata styling in the shared template-style owner instead of drawing a custom Asset Browser badge. `thumbnail_nodes.rs` emits an `asset-type-badge` panel plus a separate type label and muted status label, while `thumbnail_layout.rs` sizes the badge from the type text and information-band width. `template_style/colors/surface/variants.rs` maps `asset-type-badge` to the low-emphasis hover layer and `template_style_tests/surface.rs` locks that it draws without a border. This gives thumbnail tiles a Content Browser-like type/status row while keeping the root painter free of asset-browser-specific branches.

The 2026-06-26 text/search-field follow-up keeps input-field visual density in the text-field owner rather than authored per-instance pixels. `style_selector/workbench_text_field/palette.rs` resolves normal fields to the central Slate-like panel surface, hover to the hot neutral surface, borders to the shared field border role, and placeholders to muted text instead of disabled text. `template_fields/search.rs` owns search-specific compact paint geometry using `METRICS.row_height` plus border-derived padding, so over-tall authored search fields center a stable 28 px control while normal fields keep their authored height. `template_fields/geometry.rs` only delegates the search-specific clamp after pixel alignment and layout offsets. Focused regressions in `template_fields_tests/style.rs` lock the palette role, placeholder role, and compact search geometry before the Asset Browser search and Quick Import fields are re-composed into the wider Workbench window. The 2026-06-30 neutral-focus follow-up narrows ordinary focused chrome further: focused text/search fields now use neutral `PALETTE.surface` plus `PALETTE.border`, and `editor_workbench_strict.zui` projects `workbench_field_component_focused`/`focus_border` as `#151b1f`/`#2a3238`. `PALETTE.focus_ring` remains reserved for true keyboard focus-visible or high-signal interaction states outside this ordinary field chrome slice, so componentized pointer focus and the component atlas no longer turn text fields into cyan rectangles.

The 2026-07-06 field layer-order follow-up keeps input-field internal paint ordering in the same local owner style. `template_fields/layers.rs` now owns the search glyph, stepper, and text layer offsets used by `template_fields/commands.rs`, replacing scattered `order + 1`/`order + 2`/`order + 3` values with named private constants and a regression that keeps text above glyph and stepper commands. This is intentionally a retained text/search-field helper owner rather than a shared render contract, and it does not change runtime text shaping, font preference, or glyph rasterization routes. Validation used scoped rustfmt, a static scan for residual raw order offsets, and a repo `target` field/editor/workbench screenshot scan with no matches; focused Cargo and `docs/tests/editor` screenshot refresh remain deferred while other cargo/rustc lanes are active.

The 2026-07-06 field text min-width follow-up keeps narrow-field text command fallback in the field metrics owner. `WorkbenchFieldMetrics` now exposes `min_text_rect_width`, projected from host border width with a 1 px lower bound, and `template_fields/text.rs` uses that field for the text command rectangle width guard instead of owning a local `.max(1.0)`. This lets compact, dense, or high-border host profiles change text-field fallback behavior through the same metrics projection that already owns font size, line height, padding, search icon size, and stepper geometry. Validation used scoped rustfmt, a static scan confirming the leaf text-width guard is gone, the metrics projection assertion for `min_text_rect_width`, and a repo `target` field/editor/workbench screenshot scan with no matches; focused Cargo and `docs/tests/editor` screenshot refresh remain deferred while other cargo/rustc lanes are active.

The 2026-07-06 field paint min-extent follow-up keeps the matching geometry fallback in the field metrics owner. `WorkbenchFieldMetrics` now also exposes `min_paint_rect_extent`, projected from the same host border-width lower bound, and `template_fields/geometry.rs` plus `template_fields/search.rs` consume it for pixel-aligned field bounds and compact search-height clamps. This prevents input field geometry leaves from carrying their own fixed 1 px fallback while keeping normal authored sizes and existing search compacting behavior unchanged. Validation used scoped rustfmt, a static scan confirming the geometry/search leaf guards are gone, the metrics projection assertion for `min_paint_rect_extent`, and a repo `target` field/editor/workbench screenshot scan with no matches; focused Cargo and `docs/tests/editor` screenshot refresh remain deferred while other cargo/rustc lanes are active.

The 2026-07-06 field search glyph boundary follow-up separates search-field semantics from icon drawing. `template_fields/search.rs` remains the owner for search identity, text inset, compact paint bounds, and placeholder classification, while `template_fields/search/glyph.rs` owns the search icon asset route plus fallback ring and handle segment commands. The public retained entry point stays `push_search_field_glyph(...)`, so `template_fields/commands.rs` does not need to know whether the icon came from the shared shell SVG asset or the retained fallback projection. The 2026-07-07 surface/media/spacing component screenshot follow-up adds `zircon_editor/src/tests/host/retained_menu_pointer/surface_media_visual_screenshot.rs` as the local component-level visual evidence route for retained container, image, asset-thumbnail, and spacing primitives. The screenshot renders panel/inset/content surfaces, content-panel and asset-content recessed wells, selected asset-thumbnail-card, asset-preview-visual preview pixels, asset-placeholder-visual semantic icon pixels, a generic fitted Image node, top-level gutters, and compact child spacing blocks through the retained surface/image/asset-thumbnail painters. Validation used scoped rustfmt, focused Cargo `surface_media_spacing_component_visual_paints_layered_surfaces_images_and_gutters` 1/1 with existing warnings, ignored screenshot export `capture_surface_media_spacing_component_visual_artifact` 1/1, screenshot `docs/tests/editor/editor-components-surfaces-media-spacing-900x360.png` (28174 bytes, SHA256 `9A57CE6FD7935DE52AFC689F829D220FB0E9C78E34F93DCF432780C8C447B040`), same-name scans returning 0 matches in both repo `target` and `E:\cargo-targets`, and manual visual inspection with no obvious panel, thumbnail, image-fit, or spacing overlap. This is local surface/media/spacing evidence only; it does not declare complete drawers, Asset Browser grids, image viewer workflows, window chrome, or full-window responsive-layout completion.

The 2026-07-07 Material/feedback primitives component screenshot follow-up adds `zircon_editor/src/tests/host/retained_menu_pointer/material_feedback_visual_screenshot.rs` as the local component-level visual evidence route for Material foundation and feedback primitives. The screenshot renders Paper, outlined Paper, image and text Avatars, Badge root/overlay/dot forms, horizontal and vertical Dividers, text/rounded/circular Skeleton wave states, determinate and indeterminate Linear Progress, Circular Progress, and Backdrop overlay through the retained Material primitive and feedback painters. Validation used scoped rustfmt, focused Cargo `material_feedback_component_visual_paints_foundation_primitives` 1/1 with existing warnings, ignored screenshot export `capture_material_feedback_component_visual_artifact` 1/1, screenshot `docs/tests/editor/editor-components-material-feedback-primitives-900x360.png` (31924 bytes, SHA256 `C8404659B5E9B411A704FFD301C659FDD854B8398D3DA7DBFB8A4BC63A8B269F`), same-name scans returning 0 matches in both repo `target` and `E:\cargo-targets`, and manual visual inspection with no obvious avatar, badge, divider, skeleton/progress, backdrop, or text overlap. This is local Material/feedback primitive evidence only; it does not declare a complete Material drawer, feedback manager, overlay stack, responsive window, or full-window visual completion. The only runtime edit in the same slice was the minimal glyphon type compatibility unblock in `native_bitmap_atlas_pending_placeholder(...)`, changing the current `TextArea` parameter shape to `TextArea<'_>` without changing text shaping or raster policy.

The Quick Import placeholder follow-up keeps the empty path field in that same field-text path instead of drawing ad-hoc overlay text from the Asset Browser layout. `template_fields/text.rs` treats `AssetBrowserImportPathField` with empty `value_text` and non-empty projected label as a placeholder label, so the shared Workbench text-field style paints it with `PALETTE.text_muted`. The regression in `template_fields_tests/style.rs` also asserts that a non-empty path value returns to the normal field text color.

The 2026-06-27 content-panel surface follow-up keeps content container semantics inside the template-style owners. `template_style/surface_roles.rs` classifies `content-panel` and `asset-content`, `template_style/colors/surface/variants.rs` maps them to the recessed content layer, `template_style/colors/surface/interaction.rs` prevents hover/focus/selected state from repainting them as command or input controls, and `template_style/dimensions.rs` plus `template_style/colors/border.rs` preserve a 1 px muted outline. The regression in `template_style_tests/surface.rs` locks that selected/focused content panels remain low-emphasis containers instead of focus-ring boxes.

The 2026-06-30 content-panel shell follow-up aligns the Workbench shell panel path with that generic content-panel contract. `style_selector/workbench_chrome/palette.rs` exposes the content-panel background as the projected `PALETTE.surface_inset`, and `style_selector/workbench_chrome/fill.rs` keeps `WorkbenchChromeKind::ContentPanel` on that recessed layer across idle, hover, focus, selected, checked, open, and dragging states. `template_shell_panels` remains the frame/border paint consumer, and the component atlas now includes a visible 54 px `WorkbenchAtlasLeftPanel` content-panel sample so future visual checks can see the recessed body, 1 px border, and token gap instead of only a thin strip.
